class="article-body-module__wNr16G__museGlintVisuals article-body-module__wNr16G__prominentSectionHeadings space-y-6"><p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">Today, we&#x27;re introducing Muse Ember, our smallest and most efficient model designed for complex reasoning execution and the work agents repeat all day: tool calls, coding subtasks, routing, extraction, and long document parsing.</p>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">Muse Ember delivers more intelligence per dollar than Claude Haiku 5.5 or GPT-6 Luna at any reasoning setting: it matches Haiku&#x27;s best score at about a third of the cost and beats every Luna setting. It also sits on the efficiency frontier of the full Artificial Analysis leaderboard: no model scores higher for less per task.</p>

<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">For more details about our evaluations, see our <a class="text-link underline underline-offset-4" rel="noopener noreferrer" title="Muse Ember Evaluation Report" target="_blank" href="/static/muse-ember-evaluation-report">report</a>.</p>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">Muse Ember is available today on the Meta Model API, at $0.10 per million input tokens and $0.50 per million output tokens.</p>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">In early testing, our partners put Muse Ember to work on the kinds of tasks agents repeat all day, from reviewing contracts to writing code.</p>

class="article-body-module__wNr16G__museGlintVisuals article-body-module__wNr16G__prominentSectionHeadings space-y-6"><h2 class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__sectionHeading font-display text-text-primary pt-8 text-3xl leading-tight font-medium" data-article-section-target="true" id="agents"><span data-article-section-heading-label="true">Agents</span></h2>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">Agents spend most of their tokens on many small, structured steps, and Muse Ember is built for exactly that. It can carry a business workflow end to end, pulling records, calling tools in sequence, and updating systems across apps.</p>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">With a 1M-token context window and up to 512K output tokens, it can take in a full codebase or document set in a single call.</p>

<h2 class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__sectionHeading font-display text-text-primary pt-8 text-3xl leading-tight font-medium" data-article-section-target="true" id="coding"><span data-article-section-heading-label="true">Coding</span></h2>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">Muse Ember is built for the coding subtasks an agent sends off hundreds of times a session: reading files, running tests, making targeted fixes.</p>

<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">For the hardest problems, pair it with Muse Wonder: Muse Wonder plans, Muse Ember handles the volume.</p>
<h2 class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__sectionHeading font-display text-text-primary pt-8 text-3xl leading-tight font-medium" data-article-section-target="true" id="multimodal"><span data-article-section-heading-label="true">Multimodal</span></h2>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">Muse Ember natively understands text, images, audio, and video, so agents can read screenshots, charts, and scanned documents, or work from voice memos, meeting recordings, and video without a separate model. It can work through long real-world documents, operate a computer directly from screenshots, and pick out fine visual detail.</p>

<h2 class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__sectionHeading font-display text-text-primary pt-8 text-3xl leading-tight font-medium" data-article-section-target="true" id="safety"><span data-article-section-heading-label="true">Safety</span></h2>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">Before release, we evaluated Muse Ember under our Meta Superintelligence Scaling Framework, including chemical and biological, cybersecurity, and loss-of-control risks. Across the categories assessed, our evaluations found that Muse Ember remained below the framework’s high-risk thresholds. Though it’s a small model, it received the same core safety training and testing. We also evaluated how Muse Ember behaves when acting on users&#x27; behalf. That covers whether it respects user intent, handles sensitive information appropriately, resists instructions hidden in web pages, documents, or tool outputs that try to redirect it, and seeks human input before high-impact actions.</p>
<h2 class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__sectionHeading font-display text-text-primary pt-8 text-3xl leading-tight font-medium" data-article-section-target="true" id="availability"><span data-article-section-heading-label="true">Availability</span></h2>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">Muse Ember is available today on Muse Code and Meta Model API as <code class="bg-article-inline-code-background rounded-sm px-1 font-mono">muse-ember-1.0</code>. It costs $0.10 per million input tokens, $0.004 per million cached input tokens and $0.50 per million output tokens, with a 1M-token context window and up to 512K output tokens. Muse Ember supports the Responses API.</p>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">When we introduced Muse Wonder and Muse Wonder Full, we said we would use them to develop specialized models we can bring to everyone. Muse Ember is the first trained using Muse Wonder Full. It rounds out our portfolio of models: Muse Wonder for the hardest problems, Muse Spark for coding and agentic workflows, and Muse Ember for repeatable coding and agentic tasks at scale at about $0.14 per Artificial Analysis task, a cost low enough to run in every product, for everyone.</p>

<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">Our goal is personal superintelligence: models that help you pursue your goals, create what you imagine, deepen your relationships, and take action on what you value most. Bringing it to everyone takes more than frontier capability. It takes frontier capability at a price that scales. We have even more efficient models in training and look forward to sharing what&#x27;s next. Muse Ember, Muse Spark and Muse Wonder are now available on <a class="text-link underline underline-offset-4" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank" href="https://cloud.google.com/products/model-garden/meta">Google Cloud</a> and Oracle Cloud.</p>
<a aria-label="Try Muse Ember today on the Meta developer site" class="article-promo-module__J04j2W__button" href="https://dev.meta.ai" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">Try Muse Ember today</a>
<aside aria-label="Footnote 1" class="article-footnote-module__T9j-uW__note" id="evaluation-methodology-footnote" role="note"><span aria-hidden="true" class="article-footnote-module__T9j-uW__noteLabel">1<!-- -->.<!-- --> </span><span>All models run at max reasoning effort. Artificial Analysis results are from Intelligence Index v5, as published November 9, 2026; cost per task uses each model&#x27;s list pricing. Internal results are from Meta&#x27;s evaluation suite. The Personal Superintelligence Index (PSI) is an internal composite of eight evaluations: Agents&#x27; Last Exam, AutomationBench, FocusBench, JobBench, OfficeQA Pro, GDP.pdf, GDP.xlsx and VBT. Terminal-Bench 4.0 uses each model&#x27;s best published harness: mini-swe-agent (Muse Ember), Claude Code (Claude Haiku 5.5), and Codex (GPT-6 Luna). OSWorld 2.1 uses partial-credit reward; PerceptionBench is scored with tools.<!-- --> </span></aside>
<aside aria-label="Footnote 2" class="article-footnote-module__T9j-uW__note" id="pricing-footnote" role="note"><span aria-hidden="true" class="article-footnote-module__T9j-uW__noteLabel">2<!-- -->.<!-- --> </span><span>Pricing shown is for prompts up to 200K tokens. For longer prompts, Muse Ember costs $0.30 per million input tokens, $0.012 per million cached input tokens and $1.50 per million output tokens.<!-- -->
